          parseImportSpec rawLine

                  | isQuoted path

                [path]

                  | Just sym <- lookup c (specSingleSymbols spec)
                  = Token (TSym sym) pos : go (bump pos 1) cs
                  | specIsNumChar spec c
                  = let ... in Token (TNum num) pos : go newPos rest
                  | specIsIdentStart spec c
                  = let ... in Token tk pos : go newPos rest
                  | otherwise = go (bump pos 1) cs
                ....
    |
111 |         goMatch [] = Nothing
    |         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^...
